Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-001 · v01-full

对结果开心的重要决定

原题与完整提示

Which is better, to make a decision quickly or slowly?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

It depends on how much the decision matters. For small things, like choosing lunch, a quick decision saves time. But choosing a university or accepting a job deserves more thought. People should compare their options and ask for advice if necessary. Generally, the harder a decision is to change later, the more carefully we should make it.

中文对照

这取决于决定有多重要。像选午餐这样的事情，快速决定能节约时间。但是选大学或接受工作值得多考虑。如果有需要，人们应比较选项、征求建议。一般来说，决定日后越难改变，我们就越应该谨慎做出。

关键词

small choices · compare options · advice · hard to change

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-002 · v01-full

对结果开心的重要决定

原题与完整提示

Do you think most people's decisions are made in their 20s?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Many important decisions happen in people's twenties, but I wouldn't say most decisions do. People often choose a career and become more independent during that period. However, someone might change jobs, return to university, or move abroad much later. The twenties can shape our direction, but they don't decide everything about the rest of our lives.

中文对照

许多重要决定发生在人们二十多岁时，但我不会说多数决定都如此。在这段时期，人们常选择职业、变得更加独立。然而，一个人可能在很久以后换工作、重返大学或移居国外。二十多岁能影响我们的方向，但不会决定余生的一切。

关键词

twenties · career · independence · later changes

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-003 · v01-full

对结果开心的重要决定

原题与完整提示

What if people in a group have different opinions on a decision?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

The group should first listen to everyone's reasons rather than immediately take a vote. People may disagree because they have different priorities, such as cost or convenience. Once they share a clear goal, finding a compromise becomes easier. If there is still no agreement and time is limited, voting can be a practical final step.

中文对照

小组应该先听每个人的理由，而不是马上投票。分歧可能来自不同的优先事项，比如成本或方便程度。有了明确的共同目标，寻找折中方案会更容易。如果仍不能达成一致且时间有限，投票可以成为实际的最后一步。

关键词

listen · priorities · common goal · compromise · vote

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-004 · v01-full

对结果开心的重要决定

原题与完整提示

What little decisions do people make every day?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People make choices about food, clothes, transport, and the order of their tasks. They also decide whether to exercise or relax after studying or working. These decisions seem small, but repeated choices can become habits. For example, walking a short distance instead of taking a taxi may gradually make someone's daily routine more active.

中文对照

人们会决定吃什么、穿什么、如何出行以及任务的顺序。学习或工作后，他们也要决定锻炼还是休息。这些决定看起来很小，但重复的选择能形成习惯。例如，短距离步行而不是打车，可能逐渐让一个人的日常生活更活跃。

关键词

food · transport · task order · exercise · habits

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-005 · v01-full

对结果开心的重要决定

原题与完整提示

Do you think it is a good idea to make a small decision every day?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, making small decisions can help people become more independent. A child choosing a book, for example, gets some practice in deciding what they enjoy. Adults can also build confidence without risking very much. Still, people don't need to create extra decisions deliberately. Ordinary life already offers plenty of opportunities to make simple choices.

中文对照

是的，做小决定能帮助人们更独立。例如，孩子选书时，可以练习判断自己喜欢什么。成年人也能在风险不大的情况下建立信心。不过，人们不必刻意制造额外的决定。日常生活已经提供了很多做简单选择的机会。

关键词

independence · choose a book · confidence · small risk

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-006 · v01-full

对结果开心的重要决定

原题与完整提示

How can we tell the difference between an important and an easy decision?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Importance is about a decision's consequences, while ease is about how clear the best option is. An important choice may affect someone's career or finances. However, it can still be easy if one option obviously matches their goals. Accepting a suitable job offer is an example. So important and easy aren't necessarily opposite descriptions.

中文对照

重要性看决定的后果，容易程度看最佳选项有多清晰。重要选择可能影响一个人的职业或经济状况。但如果一个选项显然符合目标，它仍然可以很容易。接受合适的工作邀请就是例子。因此，重要和容易不一定是相反的描述。

关键词

consequences · clear option · career · job offer

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-007 · v01-full

做困难事情并成功的人

原题与完整提示

In which sectors are companies more likely to succeed in China?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

I'd expect opportunities in technology, healthcare, and services that solve everyday problems. For example, useful software or affordable care can attract customers because there is a clear need. But a promising sector doesn't guarantee success. A company still needs a reliable product, sensible prices, and a good understanding of what its customers actually want.

中文对照

我预计科技、医疗和解决日常问题的服务领域会有机会。例如，有用的软件或负担得起的照护能够吸引顾客，因为需求明确。不过，有前景的行业不保证成功。公司仍需要可靠的产品、合理价格，以及对顾客实际需求的充分了解。

关键词

technology · healthcare · clear need · reliable product

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-008 · v01-full

做困难事情并成功的人

原题与完整提示

Is it easy to start up a company?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Setting up a company may be fairly simple on paper, but building a successful business is much harder. The founders need money, customers, and people they can work with. They also have to manage uncertainty, because an idea that sounds good may not sell. I'd say getting started is only the first part of the challenge.

中文对照

成立公司的手续可能相对简单，但建立成功的业务要困难得多。创始人需要资金、顾客和可以合作的人。他们还必须应对不确定性，因为听起来不错的想法可能卖不出去。我认为，开始只是挑战的第一部分。

关键词

start up · customers · team · uncertainty

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-009 · v01-full

做困难事情并成功的人

原题与完整提示

Other than making money, what is more important for a company?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

A company needs to earn people's trust and provide something genuinely useful. If it sells poor products or treats employees badly, short-term profit may damage its future. For example, reliable technical support can persuade customers to stay. Fair treatment of staff also matters, because experienced employees often make the quality of the business possible.

中文对照

公司需要赢得信任，并提供真正有用的东西。如果销售劣质产品或苛待员工，短期利润可能损害未来。例如，可靠的技术支持能促使顾客留下。公平对待员工也很重要，因为有经验的员工往往是业务质量的保障。

关键词

trust · useful products · support · fair treatment

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-010 · v01-full

做困难事情并成功的人

原题与完整提示

Does money equal success?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Money is one sign of success, but it isn't the whole picture. Someone may earn a lot while feeling exhausted or having little time for their family. Another person may earn less but do meaningful work and keep learning. I would judge success through a mixture of financial security, personal progress, and satisfaction with daily life.

中文对照

金钱是成功的一种标志，但不是全部。有人赚很多，却感到疲惫，或者很少有时间陪家人。另一个人可能赚得较少，但从事有意义的工作并持续学习。我会综合经济安全、个人进步和日常生活满意度判断成功。

关键词

money · meaningful work · progress · satisfaction

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-011 · v01-full

做困难事情并成功的人

原题与完整提示

How can companies help protect the environment?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Companies can reduce waste in both production and packaging. They can also use energy more efficiently and make products that last longer. For a technology company, repairing devices rather than immediately replacing them could help. These changes work best when they become normal business practice, rather than a temporary campaign used only to attract attention.

中文对照

公司可以减少生产和包装中的浪费，也能提高能源使用效率，让产品更耐用。对于科技公司，修理设备而不是马上更换可能有帮助。当这些改变成为日常经营做法，而非只为吸引注意的临时活动时，效果最好。

关键词

reduce waste · energy · repair · lasting change

待确认素材与观点

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-012 · v01-full

做困难事情并成功的人

原题与完整提示

Is it difficult to do business in today's world?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

It can be difficult because customers have many choices and businesses face fast changes. A small company may compete with firms that have much bigger budgets. At the same time, online tools make it easier to reach customers and test ideas. So the environment is challenging, but people also have useful resources that weren't available before.

中文对照

做生意可能很难，因为顾客选择多，企业面对快速变化。小公司可能要与预算大得多的企业竞争。同时，网络工具让接触顾客和测试想法更容易。所以环境有挑战，但人们也拥有过去没有的有用资源。

关键词

competition · fast change · online tools · opportunities

待确认素材与观点

When do you think people are happiest?

How can the arts spread positive emotions in society?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Music and theatre can help people share feelings even when their backgrounds are different. A cheerful performance may lift people's mood, while a moving story can make them more understanding. 原题与完整提示

Do you think teachers are able to spot the unhappy children?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Teachers may notice changes such as a child becoming quiet, avoiding classmates, or losing interest in lessons. However, those signs don't always explain what is wrong. A teacher should talk gently with the child and, when appropriate, involve the parents. Observation can be helpful, but it shouldn't turn into assuming that every quiet student is unhappy.

中文对照

老师可能注意到孩子变沉默、避开同学或对课堂失去兴趣等变化。但这些表现不一定说明出了什么问题。老师应温和地与孩子交谈，适当时联系家长。观察有帮助，但不应据此认定所有安静的学生都不开心。

关键词

changes · quiet · gentle conversation · avoid assumptions

Should teachers know what children are doing all the time?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Teachers need to know enough to keep children safe, especially during lessons and school activities. But watching them constantly would be unrealistic and could limit their independence. 中文对照

老师需要掌握足够信息来保障孩子安全，尤其是课堂和学校活动期间。但持续监视不现实，也可能限制独立性。大一些的孩子也需要隐私。明确规则、定期沟通和求助渠道，比试图知道孩子一天中的每个细节更有用。

关键词

safety · independence · privacy · communication

What should people wear to a job interview?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

关键词

Why do we need to attend interviews when applying for jobs?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

An application shows someone's qualifications, but an interview reveals how they explain ideas and respond to questions. 关键词

qualifications · communication · teamwork · mutual fit

What should people do to prepare for a job interview?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

They should understand the role and learn the basic facts about the company. It also helps to prepare a few clear examples of their skills, such as solving a problem or working in a team. Practising aloud can make answers more natural. Finally, checking the location or online meeting link prevents avoidable stress on the day.

中文对照

关键词

research · examples · practise aloud · check details

Why do some people dislike being interviewed?

Do you think it is important for managers to be well organized?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, because a manager's decisions affect other people's work. If priorities and deadlines are unclear, the whole team may waste time or repeat tasks. 关键词

priorities · deadlines · responsibilities · freedom

Should parents teach their children to be organized?

What can people do to become more organized?

Are organized people more likely to be successful at work or in their studies?

Why is it important for people to be organized?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

中文对照

关键词

How can people consider others' feelings when chatting in public?

原题与完整提示

How would people usually respond to noises in your country?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

In China, I think reactions vary with the setting. People may accept noise in markets or busy restaurants, but feel annoyed by loud phone calls on public transport. 中文对照

在中国，我认为反应随场景不同。人们可能接受市场或繁忙餐馆里的噪声，却对公共交通上大声打电话感到烦。有些人忽略，有些礼貌地请求安静。住所附近反复的噪声可能导致投诉。因此，场景与持续时间影响很大。

关键词

context · public transport · ask politely · duration

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-033 · v01-full

嘈杂地

原题与完整提示

Which area is exposed to noise more, the city or the countryside?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Cities generally have more continuous noise because traffic, construction, and businesses are close together. In the countryside, there are usually fewer sources operating at the same time. However, rural places aren't always silent. Farm machinery or a local celebration can be quite loud. The main difference is often the frequency and concentration of noise rather than its existence.

中文对照

城市通常有更多持续噪声，因为交通、施工和商家集中在一起。乡村同时运转的噪声源通常较少，但乡村并非总是安静。农业机械或当地庆祝活动也能很响。主要差别往往是噪声的频率和集中程度，而非有没有噪声。

关键词

traffic · construction · machinery · frequency

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-034 · v01-full

嘈杂地

原题与完整提示

What kinds of noises are there in our life?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

There are transport noises, such as car engines and train announcements, and household sounds like appliances or renovation work. People also create noise through conversations and music. Some sounds are useful warnings, while others are simply distracting. I think whether a sound becomes a problem depends on its volume, timing, and whether people can avoid it.

中文对照

生活中有汽车发动机和火车广播等交通声，也有家电或装修等家居声音。谈话和音乐也产生噪声。有些声音是有用警示，另一些只会干扰。我认为声音是否成为问题，取决于音量、时间，以及人们能否避开。

关键词

transport · household · music · timing

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-045 · v01-full

攒钱买特别物品

原题与完整提示

Is it easier to spend money or save money?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Spending is usually easier because buying something gives an immediate reward. Saving asks people to delay that reward and resist tempting offers. Online shopping can make spending almost effortless. Still, saving becomes more manageable with a realistic budget and an automatic transfer. That way, people don't have to make the same difficult choice every time they receive money.

中文对照

花钱通常更容易，因为购买带来即时奖励。储蓄要求推迟奖励、抵抗诱人优惠。网购几乎让花钱毫不费力。不过，现实预算和自动转账能让储蓄更好管理。这样，每次收到钱时不必反复做同样困难的选择。

关键词

immediate reward · temptation · budget · automatic transfer

原题与完整提示

Should parents teach their children to spend money freely or to save money?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Parents should teach children to balance spending and saving rather than encourage either extreme. Children need to enjoy some of their money, but also understand needs, wants, and future goals. 中文对照

关键词

balance · needs and wants · allowance · saving goal

Should schools teach children how to manage money from a young age?

关键词

Can a child learn skills from another child?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, children often learn by watching and copying one another. A child who understands a game or a simple computer task can explain it in familiar language. 关键词

Do you think it is easier for children to learn new skills than for adults?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

关键词

play · mistakes · experience · programming

What are the differences between the way adults learn and the way children learn?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Adults often want to know why a skill is useful before investing their time in it. They may use plans, instructions, and previous knowledge. What are the differences between learning in a group and learning by yourself?

Some people don't mind others walking into their shot when they're taking photos.

关键词

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-056 · v01-full

喜欢拍照的人

原题与完整提示

Why do tourists like taking photos while they are travelling?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Photos help tourists keep memories of places they may never visit again. They can also share the experience with family and friends who stayed at home. Sometimes taking a photo encourages people to notice interesting details, such as a building's design. Still, if they photograph everything, they may spend more time looking at a screen than enjoying the trip.

中文对照

照片帮助游客保留可能不会再去的地方的记忆，也能把经历分享给留在家的亲友。有时拍照促使人留意有趣细节，比如建筑设计。但如果什么都拍，可能看屏幕的时间多于享受旅行的时间。

关键词

memories · share · details · enjoy the trip

喜欢拍照的人

原题与完整提示

Do you think it’s ok to take photos everywhere?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

No, some places need clear limits. Hospitals may protect patients' privacy, and museums may restrict photography to protect objects or improve the visitor experience. People should also ask before taking close photos of strangers. Being able to take a picture doesn't automatically mean it is appropriate. Respecting signs and people's comfort should come before getting an interesting shot.

中文对照

不，有些地方需要明确限制。医院可能要保护患者隐私，博物馆可能限制摄影以保护展品或改善参观体验。近距离拍陌生人前也应询问。能拍照不自动代表合适。遵守标识、尊重人的舒适感，应先于得到有趣照片。

关键词

privacy · museum rules · ask first · respect

What kind of photos do people often take?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People often photograph family, friends, food, and places they visit. Some pictures record important occasions, while others capture ordinary moments that seem worth remembering. 原题与完整提示

Why are some people keen on taking photos?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

For some people, photography is a creative hobby. They enjoy choosing the light, angle, and composition rather than simply recording an event. Others like keeping memories or sharing experiences online. It can also be satisfying to notice something beautiful that everyone else overlooks. Different motives can exist together, so a keen photographer doesn't necessarily take photos only for attention.

中文对照

对有些人，摄影是创造性爱好。他们享受选择光线、角度和构图，而非只是记录事件。另一些人喜欢保存记忆或网上分享经历。发现别人忽略的美也令人满足。不同动机能并存，因此热衷摄影者未必只为获取关注。

关键词

creativity · light and angle · memories · notice beauty

When do people like taking photos?

原题与完整提示

What kinds of things do the elderly remember for a long time?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Older people may remember emotionally important moments, such as childhood experiences, family events, and their first job. Familiar places and friendships can also remain vivid. These memories often carry a personal meaning, rather than being random facts. However, everyone is different, and I wouldn't assume that all older people remember the same things or forget recent events.

中文对照

年长者可能记得情感重要的时刻，比如童年经历、家庭事件和第一份工作。熟悉的地方与友谊也可能记忆鲜明。这些记忆往往有个人意义，而非随意事实。不过每个人不同，我不会假设所有老人记得同样事情或忘记近期事件。

关键词

childhood · family events · first job · personal meaning

原题与完整提示

What sports are popular among older people?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Walking, tai chi, and gentle group exercise are familiar choices among older people in China. They don't necessarily require expensive equipment and can provide social contact as well as movement. Some older people enjoy swimming or cycling too. The suitable activity depends on the person's health and experience, so age alone shouldn't decide what they are allowed to enjoy.

中文对照

在中国，散步、太极和温和集体锻炼是年长者常见选择。它们未必需要昂贵装备，既提供活动，也提供社交。有些老人也喜欢游泳或骑行。合适活动取决于健康与经验，因此不应仅凭年龄决定允许他们享受什么。

关键词

walking · tai chi · social contact · health

What do old people usually do in their daily life?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Their routines vary a lot. Retired people may cook, exercise, meet friends, or help with family responsibilities. Some keep working, study new subjects, or enjoy hobbies they previously had little time for. I think it's more useful to consider their interests and circumstances than imagine one typical routine. Retirement can create flexibility, but it doesn't make everyone's life identical.

尊敬的比你年长的人

原题与完整提示

Why is there a generation gap between the young and the old?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Different generations grow up with different technology, social expectations, and opportunities. That can influence how they think about work, relationships, or money. Young people may see a new tool as normal while older people find it unfamiliar. But a gap doesn't have to become a conflict. Patient conversation can help both sides understand the experiences behind their opinions.

中文对照

不同代人在不同技术、社会期待和机会中成长，影响他们对工作、关系或金钱的看法。年轻人可能觉得新工具很正常，年长者却陌生。但差异不必变成冲突。耐心交谈能帮助双方理解观点背后的经历。

关键词

technology · expectations · experience · conversation

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Has old people's life quality improved when compared to the past?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

In many ways, there are more possibilities now, such as better communication tools and improved access to services. These can make daily life more convenient for older people. So better technology and facilities help, but support must remain accessible to people with different needs.

尊敬的比你年长的人

原题与完整提示

What can young and old people learn from each other?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Young people can learn patience and practical judgment from older people's experience. Older people may learn about new technology or current ways of studying and working. This exchange works best when neither side acts superior. For example, helping someone use a phone can become a conversation about both modern tools and how they solved problems before those tools existed.

中文对照

年轻人能从年长者经验中学耐心和实际判断。年长者可能了解新技术或当下学习工作的方式。双方都不摆优越姿态时，交流最好。例如，帮人用手机，也能变成关于现代工具及没有这些工具时如何解题的交谈。

关键词

experience · technology · mutual respect · exchange

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-067 · v01-full

好的购物服务

原题与完整提示

Can consumption drive economic growth?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, when people buy goods and services, businesses receive income and may hire more staff or expand production. That supports other businesses too, such as suppliers. However, spending beyond people's means isn't healthy growth. I think consumption helps most when it meets real needs and is supported by stable income, rather than pressure to keep buying unnecessary things.

中文对照

是的，人们购买商品服务时，企业获得收入，可能招更多员工或扩大生产。这也支持供应商等其他企业。但超出支付能力的消费不是健康增长。我认为消费在满足真实需求、有稳定收入支持时最有帮助，而非受压不断买不必要东西。

关键词

income · jobs · suppliers · real needs

好的购物服务

原题与完整提示

What are the advantages and disadvantages of shopping online?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Online shopping makes it easy to compare prices and find products that local shops may not stock. It is convenient when people are busy. The disadvantage is that they can't inspect the item before paying, and returns may take time. Reviews help, but they aren't always reliable. For expensive or highly personal purchases, extra care is sensible.

中文对照

网购方便比较价格、找到本地商店可能不卖的产品，忙碌时也方便。缺点是付款前无法检查实物，退货可能耗时。评论有帮助，但不总可靠。对于昂贵或非常个人化的购买，多谨慎些是合理的。

关键词

compare prices · choice · inspection · returns

原题与完整提示

What are the differences between online shopping and in-store shopping?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Online shopping usually offers more choice and quick price comparisons, but buyers have to wait for delivery. In a physical shop, they can examine a product and ask questions immediately. That is useful for clothes or equipment where fit and feel matter. I see the two as different tools, with the better option determined by the purchase.

中文对照

网购通常选择更多、方便比价，但买家必须等配送。实体店可以现场检查产品、马上提问。当衣服或设备的合身程度、触感重要时，这很有用。我把两者视为不同工具，更好选择由具体购买决定。

关键词

choice · delivery · try products · purchase type

原题与完整提示

Why do some people not like shopping in small shops?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Small shops may have a limited range of products, and prices can be harder to compare. Some customers also worry about returns or product consistency. A larger store may feel more predictable. However, that doesn't mean small shops provide poor service. A reliable local shop can offer personal advice and convenience that a large chain doesn't always provide.

中文对照

小店产品范围可能有限，价格也较难比较。有些顾客还担心退货或产品一致性，大店可能感觉更可预期。但这不代表小店服务差。可靠的本地小店能提供个性建议与便利，大连锁未必总能做到。

关键词

limited choice · returns · predictability · personal service

原题与完整提示

What are the advantages and disadvantages of shopping in small shops?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Small shops can be convenient, friendly, and familiar with their regular customers. They may also sell distinctive local products. On the other hand, their stock and opening hours can be limited, and prices aren't always the lowest. I would choose one for a simple local purchase, but compare more widely when buying something expensive or technically complicated.

中文对照

小店方便友好，熟悉常客，也可能售有特色的本地产品。另一方面，库存和营业时间可能有限，价格未必最低。我会用小店完成简单本地购买，买昂贵或技术复杂的东西时，则更广泛比较。

关键词

local shop · regular customers · stock · compare

Why are shopping malls so popular in China?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Shopping malls combine shops, restaurants, cinemas, and other activities in one place. That makes them convenient for families or friends with different interests. 中文对照

Does art, like music or theater, have an impact on society?

Is it good to have creative people in a team?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Creative people can suggest approaches that others haven't considered, especially when a team gets stuck. In a technical project, an unusual idea may lead to a better design. What impact can artistic buildings have on society?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

An attractive building can give a place a recognizable identity and encourage people to visit. Public buildings may also make everyday spaces more enjoyable. However, appearance alone isn't enough. Do children these days still like doing arts and crafts?

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-077 · v01-full

擅长做手工的人

原题与完整提示

Why do some children enjoy making things by hand?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Making something by hand gives children control over their ideas. They can choose colours, shapes, and details, then see the result of their own effort. It is also different from simply watching a screen. Some enjoy the process, while others feel proud of the finished object. Small successes can encourage them to attempt more difficult projects later.

中文对照

手工制作让孩子掌控自己的想法，可以选颜色、形状和细节，然后看到努力成果。这也不同于只看屏幕。有些享受过程，另一些为成品自豪。小成功能鼓励日后尝试更难的项目。

关键词

control · colours · pride · small successes

Will you continue doing something when you are aware that it’s a waste of time?

Do you think stress is important in people's lives?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

关键词

学习并喜欢历史的人

原题与完整提示

Besides big historical events, what else is important when learning history?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Everyday life is important too: how people worked, ate, learned, and raised families. It helps us understand what major events meant for ordinary people. We should also ask where historical information comes from and whose viewpoint it represents. Dates provide a framework, but understanding causes, consequences, and different experiences makes history much more meaningful than memorizing a list of events.

中文对照

日常生活也重要：人们怎样工作、吃饭、学习、养育家庭。这帮助理解重大事件对普通人意味着什么。也应问历史信息来自哪里、代表谁的视角。日期提供框架，但理解原因、后果与不同经历，比背事件清单更有意义。

关键词

daily life · sources · viewpoints · causes

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-098 · v01-full

学习并喜欢历史的人

原题与完整提示

Do you think people should learn ancient history before learning modern history?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Learning ancient history first can provide useful background, especially when later institutions developed from earlier ones. But a strict order isn't always necessary. A modern event may interest students and lead them to explore its older roots. I think a clear timeline and links between periods matter more than requiring everyone to begin with the oldest material.

中文对照

先学古代史能提供背景，尤其后来的制度源自早期制度。但严格顺序不总必要。现代事件可能吸引学生，促使探索更早根源。我认为清晰时间线和时期联系，比要求所有人从最古老材料开始更重要。

关键词

background · modern interest · roots · timeline

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-099 · v01-full

学习并喜欢历史的人

原题与完整提示

Is it important to learn how people in the past lived their lives?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, it shows that history is about real people, not just rulers and wars. Understanding their daily challenges can make past events easier to relate to. It also helps us notice what has changed and what remains familiar. For example, the tools people used for work reveal both technological progress and the effort behind ordinary life.

中文对照

是的，这表明历史关乎真实的人，不只是统治者和战争。理解日常挑战能让过去事件更容易产生联系，也帮助注意改变与仍熟悉之处。例如，过去工作工具既体现技术进步，也体现普通生活背后的努力。

关键词

real people · daily challenges · change · tools

How do children learn history before they attend school?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

They can learn through family stories, picture books, museum visits, or child-friendly programmes. At that age, a story about how people lived is easier to understand than a long list of dates. Adults should explain what is factual and what is fictional. The aim is to build curiosity and a simple sense of time, rather than test detailed historical knowledge.

中文对照

关键词

family stories · picture books · facts · curiosity

At what age do you think people should begin to learn history?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

I think basic history can begin around the early primary-school years, using stories and pictures. Children don't need to study difficult political arguments immediately. As they get older, they can learn timelines and compare different sources. The right starting point is when they can understand that life was different before they were born and become curious about those differences.

中文对照

我认为基础历史可在小学低年级左右通过故事和图片开始。孩子不必马上学复杂政治论争。年龄大些后可学时间线、比较不同来源。适合开始的时点，是能理解出生之前的生活不同，并开始好奇这些差异。

关键词

early school · stories · timelines · curiosity

Do you think children should begin learning history from an early age?

教你新技能的人

原题与完整提示

Why is self-discipline important when learning a new skill?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

A new skill usually requires repeated practice after the first excitement has faded. Self-discipline helps people continue when progress is slow or mistakes become frustrating. For example, learning to code involves testing and fixing problems, not just watching a tutorial. Regular, manageable practice builds ability more reliably than one long session followed by several weeks of doing nothing.

中文对照

新技能通常需要在最初兴奋消退后反复练习。自律帮助在进步缓慢或错误令人烦时坚持。例如学编程需要测试、修问题，而非只看教程。规律、可管理练习，比一次长练后数周不练更可靠地建立能力。

关键词

practice · slow progress · coding · consistency

What are the differences between learning from a teacher and learning by oneself?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

A teacher can provide structure, spot mistakes, and explain difficult ideas in a different way. Independent learning gives people flexibility and lets them follow their own interests. The risk is getting stuck without knowing what is wrong. For many skills, a combination works best: use good materials independently, then seek feedback when progress slows or a problem becomes confusing.

中文对照

老师能提供结构、发现错误、换方式解释难点。自学给灵活性，允许追随兴趣。风险是卡住却不知道错在哪里。许多技能最好结合：独立用好材料，再在进步减慢或问题令人困惑时寻求反馈。

关键词

structure · flexibility · mistakes · feedback

How do adults learn a new skill?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Adults often start by identifying a goal, then choose a course, tutorial, or person who can guide them. They usually need to fit practice around other responsibilities. How can parents and teachers teach children new skills?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

They should demonstrate the skill, let children try it, and give specific feedback. Breaking a task into small steps prevents it from feeling overwhelming. Do people’s movie preferences change as they get older?

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-110 · v01-full

不喜欢的电影

原题与完整提示

Should documentary films be completely truthful?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Documentaries should be honest about the facts they present. They can select and organize material, because no film can include everything, but they shouldn't invent events or mislead the audience through editing. If a scene is reconstructed, that should be clear. Viewers need to distinguish evidence from interpretation so they can trust the film without treating one perspective as the whole story.

中文对照

纪录片应诚实呈现事实。可以选择组织材料，因为电影不可能包含一切，但不应编事件或通过剪辑误导。若场景是重现，应明确。观众需区分证据与解读，才能信任影片又不把一个视角当全部。

关键词

facts · editing · reconstruction · interpretation

  • 观点是否符合用户看法待确认；尚未作答或录音验证

Can movies help people better understand the cultural background of a country?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, movies can show everyday details such as food, family relationships, and ways of speaking. That makes culture feel more concrete than a short description. It is a useful starting point, especially when viewers also read or talk to people from that background.

关键词

daily details · culture · director · starting point

Are movies more likely to help people become more creative than books?

Using both can give people a broader set of ideas and ways of expressing them.

What are the differences between watching movies at home and in a cinema?

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-115 · v01-full

不喜欢的短途旅行

原题与完整提示

What problems does it cause for transportation when everyone commutes at the same time?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

When many people travel at once, roads become congested and public transport gets crowded. People may wait longer, struggle to board, or arrive late. The pressure is concentrated into a short period even if capacity is available later. Flexible starting times and reliable public transport can spread the demand, reducing the need for everyone to use the same route simultaneously.

中文对照

很多人同时出行时，道路拥堵、公交拥挤。人们可能等更久、难上车或迟到。即使稍后有运力，压力也集中短时间。灵活开始时间与可靠公交可分散需求，降低大家同时用同一路线的需要。

关键词

congestion · crowding · peak demand · flexible hours

Which is better for knowing more about a country? Travelling or reading books about it?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Travelling is better for firsthand experience, while books are often better for background and explanation. A visitor can see everyday behaviour but may not understand its history or meaning. Reading can fill those gaps, although it can't fully reproduce being there. I would combine them: learn some context before a trip, then use the experience to ask more thoughtful questions.

中文对照

旅行更适合亲身体验，书更适合背景解释。访客可看日常行为，却未必理解历史意义。阅读补缺，但无法完全复制身临其境。我会结合：出发前学背景，再用旅行体验提出更深入的问题。

关键词

firsthand · background · history · combine

受欢迎的人

原题与完整提示

Why do people want to be popular?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Being popular can make people feel accepted and valued. It may also bring social or professional opportunities, because others are more willing to involve them. However, constant attention can create pressure to maintain an image. I think wanting good relationships is natural, but chasing approval from everyone can distract people from their own interests and the friendships that actually matter.

中文对照

受欢迎让人觉得被接纳、被重视，也可能带来社交职业机会，因为他人更愿让他们参与。但持续关注会带来维持形象的压力。我觉得想有好关系自然，但追求人人认可会分散对自身兴趣和重要友谊的注意。

关键词

acceptance · opportunities · image · real friendships

What are the qualities of being a good teacher?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Why are there so few top athletes?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Reaching the highest level requires an unusual combination of ability, training, support, and opportunity. Many people are talented, but injuries or limited resources can interrupt their progress. Elite competition is also demanding because the differences between athletes can be tiny. That is why enjoying a sport and becoming one of its best performers are very different goals.

中文对照

达到最高水平需要能力、训练、支持和机会的少见组合。许多人有天赋，但受伤或资源有限会中断进步。顶尖竞争要求高，因为运动员间差距可能极小。这就是享受运动和成为最好选手是非常不同目标的原因。

关键词

ability · training · support · opportunity

Is it easy to identify children’s talents?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

原题与完整提示

Is talent important in sports?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Talent can make certain movements or techniques easier to learn, particularly at a competitive level. But it doesn't remove the need for practice, discipline, and good coaching. For ordinary people, the main value of sport is participation and health, so exceptional natural ability shouldn't be a requirement.

中文对照

关键词

talent · practice · discipline · participation

原题与完整提示

Why is it hard to protect historic buildings?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Old materials may need specialized repairs, which can be expensive and slow. Owners also face pressure to use valuable land for new development. A building may need modern safety features without losing its original character. These competing demands make protection difficult. Clear funding, skilled workers, and a realistic plan for how the building will be used can help.

中文对照

旧材料可能需专业修复，昂贵且缓慢。业主也面对把高价值土地用于新发展的压力。建筑可能需现代安全设施，却要保留原貌。这些竞争需求令保护困难。明确资金、熟练工人、现实用途计划能帮助。

关键词

specialist repair · cost · land pressure · safe use

原题与完整提示

Do you think it’s a waste of money for governments to celebrate national events?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Public celebrations can build a sense of connection and help people remember important events. They may also support cultural activities that many residents enjoy. But spending should be reasonable and transparent. An expensive display that neglects basic services is hard to justify. I would support celebrations that are accessible and meaningful, with costs proportionate to their public value.

中文对照

公共庆祝能建立连接感、帮助记住重要事件，也支持许多居民享受的文化活动。但开支应合理透明。忽略基本服务的昂贵展示难辩护。我会支持可达、有意义、费用与公共价值相称的庆祝。

关键词

connection · culture · reasonable spending · public value

What effect does stress have on health?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Short periods of stress may make people more alert, but ongoing stress can interfere with sleep, concentration, and healthy routines. 关键词

原题与完整提示

Why are different places laid out and designed differently?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Places have different climates, geography, histories, and practical needs. A dense city centre may prioritize transport and limited land, while a rural area has more space. Local culture also influences what feels familiar or attractive. Good planning responds to these conditions rather than copying one fashionable layout everywhere. Similar buildings can work very differently when their surroundings and users are different.

中文对照

地方气候、地理、历史和需求不同。密集中心可能优先交通与有限土地，农村空间更多。本地文化影响熟悉美感。好规划应回应条件，而非到处复制时尚布局。周围环境和使用者不同，相似建筑表现也很不同。

关键词

climate · geography · density · local needs

喜欢或不喜欢的高建筑

原题与完整提示

What are the advantages of living in tall buildings?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Tall buildings can provide housing close to jobs and services without spreading the city over as much land. Upper floors may offer views and less direct street noise. Shared facilities can also be convenient. However, these benefits rely on good maintenance and reliable lifts. Height itself doesn't guarantee a pleasant home; layout, safety, and the surrounding neighbourhood remain important.

中文对照

高楼让住房近工作服务，不必让城市占同样多土地。高层可能有景观、少直接街声。共享设施也方便。但优势依赖维护与可靠电梯。高度本身不保证好家，布局、安全和社区仍重要。

关键词

land efficiency · views · shared facilities · maintenance

原题与完整提示

Why do some people like to remodel and decorate their homes themselves?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Designing a home themselves gives people control over the colours, layout, and details that affect daily life. It can reflect their taste more closely and create a sense of achievement. Some also enjoy the process or want to manage costs. But major structural or technical work requires appropriate expertise, so doing everything personally isn't always the safest or most economical approach.

中文对照

自己设计住房让人掌控影响日常的色彩、布局和细节，更贴合品味并带来成就。有些也享受过程或想控费用。但重大结构或技术工作需专业能力，因此全亲自做未必最安全经济。

关键词

personal taste · control · achievement · expertise

What kind of interior design style do most people like?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Comfortable, practical designs with enough light and storage seem broadly appealing. Some people like a simple modern look, while others prefer warmer traditional details. I wouldn't assume there is one style that most people want. Budget, home size, and daily routines shape the choice. A room that works well for its residents matters more than following the latest decorating trend.

中文对照

舒适实用、光线储物足够的设计似乎广有吸引力。有些喜欢简洁现代风，另一些喜欢温暖传统细节。我不假定多数人统一想一种风格。预算、住房大小、日常塑造选择。适合住户的房间，比跟装饰潮流重要。

关键词

有趣视频

原题与完整提示

Which is more helpful, watching videos or reading books?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Videos are especially helpful when a skill involves movement or a visible sequence, such as cooking or assembling something. Books can explain background ideas in more detail and make it easy to return to a particular section. For technical learning, I would use a video to see the process, then written material to understand it and check details.

中文对照

技能含动作或可视顺序时，如烹饪或组装，视频尤其有用。书能更详细解释背景概念，方便回到具体部分。技术学习中，我会用视频看过程，再用书面材料理解和核对细节。

关键词

demonstration · background · details · combine resources

原题与完整提示

What skills can people learn from watching videos?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People can learn practical skills such as cooking, drawing, repairing simple items, or using software. Videos can show the order of actions and common mistakes clearly. They may also introduce programming ideas, but watching alone isn't enough to become skilled. Viewers need to practise, test their understanding, and use reliable guidance when a task involves safety or expensive equipment.

中文对照

人们能学烹饪、绘画、修简单物件、使用软件等实用技能。视频清楚展示动作顺序与常错，也能介绍编程概念，但只看不足以熟练。观众需练习、检验理解；涉及安全或昂贵设备时用可靠指导。

关键词

cooking · software · practice · reliable guidance

有趣视频

原题与完整提示

What makes a video go viral online?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

A video often spreads when it creates a strong reaction, such as surprise, amusement, or recognition. A clear idea that people understand quickly is easier to share. Timing and platform recommendations can help too. However, attention is unpredictable, and a viral video isn't necessarily accurate or valuable. I think shareability explains popularity better than simply having expensive production.

中文对照

视频带来惊讶、好笑或认同等强反应时常传播。易快速理解的清晰想法更易分享。时机和平台推荐也有用。但注意难预测，爆款不一定准确有价值。我认为易分享比昂贵制作更能解释流行。

关键词

emotion · clear idea · sharing · timing

去过的无聊地方

原题与完整提示

Why do most children think education is boring?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Lessons can feel boring when children only listen, memorize, and repeat information without seeing its purpose. Work that is too easy or too difficult can also reduce interest. Practical activities and meaningful questions may help students become more involved. I think the problem is often how learning is presented, rather than children having no curiosity about the world.

中文对照

孩子只听、背、复述信息而看不到目的时，课可能无聊。太容易或太难的任务也降低兴趣。实践活动和有意义问题帮助参与。我认为问题常在呈现学习的方式，而非孩子对世界无好奇。

关键词

purpose · active learning · difficulty · curiosity

原题与完整提示

Why aren’t young people willing to listen to the experiences of older people?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Young people may feel that older people's experiences don't match the world they live in, especially when technology or work has changed. Advice can also sound like criticism if it isn't invited. However, practical wisdom may still be relevant. Older people can explain without lecturing, and younger people can ask questions before deciding whether an experience offers something useful.

中文对照

年轻人可能觉得老人的经历与当下世界不符，尤其技术工作改变时。未经请求的建议也可能像批评。但实际智慧仍相关。老人可解释而非训话，年轻人可先提问，再判断经历是否有用。

关键词

changed world · unsolicited advice · wisdom · questions

What can people do when they feel bored?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People can change what they are doing rather than keep scrolling on their phones. A short walk, a conversation or a small practical task can make a difference. 中文对照

人们可以换件事做，而不是一直刷手机。散步、聊天或完成一项小任务，都可能有所帮助。有时无聊也意味着当前活动缺乏意义，因此选择有明确目标的事情，比如学习技能，可能比单纯娱乐更有效。

原题与完整提示

Why are some teachers’ classes boring? Are there any solutions?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Some classes are boring because the teacher talks for too long without involving students. The material may also feel disconnected from real life. Teachers could use shorter explanations, practical examples and small discussions. However, entertainment alone is not enough; students still need to understand the subject and have a chance to ask questions.

中文对照

有些课无聊，是因为老师讲得太久，没有让学生参与。教材也可能与实际生活脱节。老师可以缩短讲解，增加实际例子和小组讨论。不过，仅仅有趣还不够，学生仍需理解内容，并有机会提问。

关键词

involve students · practical examples · ask questions

Do most people think news about celebrities is boring?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

I would not assume that most people find it boring. Some enjoy following actors or singers because they admire their work. 关键词

Do you know anyone who likes to get up early?

Why do people get up early?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People get up early for practical reasons, such as work, school or a long journey. Others choose to exercise before their responsibilities begin. 关键词

What kinds of occasions need people to arrive early?

Why do some people like to stay up late?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Some people feel more alert at night, especially when the surroundings become quiet. Others stay up because of work, entertainment or conversations with friends. 关键词

feel more alert · enough sleep · a regular schedule

原题与完整提示

Is it good to arrive early in any situation?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Not in every situation. Being early is sensible for an examination or a train, because missing it has clear consequences. But arriving much earlier than agreed at someone's home may inconvenience the host. I think punctuality means considering the situation and other people's schedules, not always trying to be the first person there.

中文对照

并非任何场合都适合。考试或赶火车时提前到达很合理，因为错过会有明确后果。但去别人家时比约定早很多，可能会给主人添麻烦。我认为守时应考虑具体情况和他人的安排，而不是总想第一个到。

关键词

clear consequences · inconvenience the host · punctuality

What are the advantages of growing vegetables or flowers at home?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Growing plants can make a home more attractive and give people a relaxing activity away from screens. Vegetables may also provide a small amount of fresh food. 中文对照

种植物可以让家更好看，也提供远离屏幕、放松身心的活动。蔬菜还能带来少量新鲜食物。对孩子而言，观察植物生长让耐心和责任感更容易理解。不过，种植需要空间和照料，收益取决于家庭能够承担的程度。

关键词

away from screens · fresh food · patience and responsibility

Do many people grow vegetables or flowers at home in your country?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Some people in China do grow flowers or vegetables at home, but the available space makes a big difference. A house with a garden allows larger plants, while apartment residents might keep a few pots on a balcony. I would not claim that everyone does it; personal interest, time and sunlight all affect the choice.

中文对照

中国确实有人在家种花或种菜，但可用空间影响很大。有花园的房子能种较大的植物，公寓居民可能只在阳台放几个花盆。我不会说人人都这样做，个人兴趣、时间和光照都会影响选择。

关键词

available space · a few pots · sunlight

Is it easy to grow plants at home?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

中文对照

关键词

a hardy plant · appropriate watering · regular care

关键词

原题与完整提示

Do you think doctors should be paid more?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Doctors should receive fair pay that reflects their training, workload and responsibility. Where conditions are difficult or staffing is inadequate, better pay may help retain skilled people. But money alone will not solve every problem. Reasonable working hours, professional support and safe conditions are also necessary if we want doctors to provide consistently good care.

中文对照

医生应获得与培训、工作量和责任相匹配的公平薪酬。条件困难或人员不足时，提高收入可能帮助留住人才，但钱无法解决所有问题。合理工时、专业支持和安全条件也不可缺少，才能让医生持续提供良好照护。

关键词

fair pay · retain skilled people · working hours

Should governments provide financial support to start-ups?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

关键词

What makes a business fail?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

A business may fail when it offers something customers do not really need or cannot afford. Poor cash management and expanding too quickly can create problems as well. 中文对照

关键词

cash management · expand too quickly · test the idea

How does technology help people make plans?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

关键词

Should students learn to do group work?

share responsibilities · handle disagreement · individual contributions

Who can children turn to for help when making a decision?

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-231 · v01-full

重要决定

原题与完整提示

Do you think advertisements can influence our decisions when shopping?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, advertisements can make a product more familiar and highlight benefits we might not have considered. Repeated exposure may also make a brand feel trustworthy even before we try it. That does not mean the product is the best choice. I think shoppers should compare useful information, prices and actual needs rather than rely only on the advert.

中文对照

会。广告让产品更熟悉，也突出我们未曾考虑的优点。反复接触还可能让品牌在试用前就显得可信，但这不意味着它就是最佳选择。我认为购物者应比较有效信息、价格和实际需要，而非只依赖广告。

关键词

repeated exposure · feel trustworthy · actual needs

重要决定

原题与完整提示

Do you think the influence of advertising is good?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

It can be positive when advertising helps people discover a useful product or understand its features. The problem comes when it exaggerates results or creates pressure to buy unnecessary things. Children may be particularly vulnerable to that pressure. So I would support honest, clear advertising, alongside consumer education and reasonable limits on misleading claims.

中文对照

广告帮助人们发现有用产品、理解功能时，影响可以是积极的。夸大效果、制造购买不必要物品的压力时，就会出问题，孩子尤其容易受影响。因此，我支持诚实清楚的广告，也重视消费教育和对误导宣传的合理限制。

关键词

useful features · misleading claims · consumer education

原题与完整提示

How do people usually make important decisions?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People often start by identifying the options and considering possible consequences. They may gather information, discuss the choice with someone they trust and compare short-term and long-term effects. Emotions matter as well, but I would avoid deciding only under pressure. For an important decision, allowing time to check assumptions can prevent a mistake that is difficult to reverse.

中文对照

人们通常先找出选项、考虑后果，再收集信息，与可信的人讨论，并比较短期和长期影响。情绪也重要，但我会避免只在压力下作决定。面对重要选择，花时间检查假设，可以避免难以挽回的错误。

关键词

possible consequences · check assumptions · difficult to reverse

关键词

原题与完整提示

What are the differences between everyday food and festival food?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Everyday food is usually chosen for convenience, nutrition and cost. Festival food may involve more preparation, a particular tradition or dishes that are shared with a larger group. It can feel special because people do not eat it so often. However, the distinction is not absolute; a familiar simple dish can also become meaningful on a celebration day.

中文对照

日常食物通常考虑方便、营养和成本。节日食物可能准备更费工、承载传统，或适合更多人分享。因为不常吃，它会显得特别。不过，界限并不绝对，熟悉简单的菜也能在庆祝日具有特别意义。

关键词

convenience and nutrition · more preparation · a particular tradition

Are there any differences between the food people eat today and the food people ate in the past?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Many people now have access to a wider range of ingredients and meals from different places. Refrigeration, transport and online delivery can also change how food reaches the table. 中文对照

如今很多人能接触更丰富的食材和各地菜肴。冷藏、运输和线上配送也改变食物上桌的方式。过去的选择往往更依赖本地和季节供应。不过，传统菜仍然重要，变化并不意味着旧饮食习惯直接消失。

关键词

a wider range · seasonal supplies · traditional dishes

Why is it easier for children to learn new things than for adults?

原题与完整提示

How do people learn new things?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People usually combine instruction, observation and practice. A clear explanation provides a starting point, but trying the task reveals what they do not yet understand. Feedback then helps them improve. For example, learning to programme requires actually writing and testing code. I think small, repeated attempts are often more effective than consuming large amounts of information without using it.

中文对照

人们通常结合讲解、观察和练习。清楚的说明提供起点，实际尝试则暴露尚未理解的部分，反馈帮助继续改进。例如，学编程需要真正写代码并测试。我认为小规模重复尝试，往往比大量接收却不使用信息更有效。

关键词

instruction and practice · feedback · repeated attempts

擅长学习和说语言的人

原题与完整提示

What is the most important thing for learning a language well?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

I think the most important thing is using the language regularly in meaningful situations. Memorising words helps, but learners also need to listen, speak, read and write with a purpose. Feedback is useful because repeated mistakes can become habits. A routine that is realistic and sustainable usually matters more than studying intensely for a few days and then stopping.

中文对照

我认为最重要的是在有意义的情境中规律使用语言。背词有帮助，但也需有目的地听、说、读、写。反馈很有用，因为重复错误可能形成习惯。现实、能持续的规律，通常比猛学几天后停止更重要。

关键词

meaningful situations · feedback · a sustainable routine

遇到的科技问题

原题与完整提示

What are the advantages and disadvantages of AI?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

AI can process information quickly and help with repetitive tasks, which gives people more time for complex work. It can also support learning and accessibility. However, its output may be inaccurate or biased, and collecting data can raise privacy concerns. I think the benefits depend on careful design, human checking and clear responsibility when the system makes mistakes.

中文对照

AI能快速处理信息、帮助完成重复任务，为复杂工作留出时间，也能支持学习和无障碍使用。但输出可能不准确或有偏见，数据收集也可能引发隐私问题。我认为收益取决于谨慎设计、人工检查，以及系统出错时明确的责任。

关键词

repetitive tasks · inaccurate or biased · human checking

原题与完整提示

Do you think people today should learn about AI technology?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, people should understand the basic capabilities and limits of AI, even if they do not become specialists. They need to know that a confident answer can still be wrong and that personal information deserves care. Basic knowledge helps them decide when a tool is useful, when to check its output and when a human should remain responsible.

中文对照

应该，即使不成为专家，也应了解AI的基本能力和局限。人们需要知道，自信的回答仍可能出错，个人信息也应谨慎处理。基础知识帮助判断何时工具有用、何时核查输出，以及何时应由人继续承担责任。

关键词

capabilities and limits · check the output · personal information

Should children learn to use AI?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Children can learn to use suitable AI tools with guidance, but they should first develop their own thinking and basic skills. A tool might explain a difficult idea or suggest practice, rather than complete every assignment for them. Teachers and parents need to discuss accuracy and privacy. The goal should be learning with assistance, not avoiding the effort of learning.

中文对照

孩子可以在指导下使用合适的AI工具，但首先应发展独立思考和基础技能。工具可以解释难点、建议练习，而不是包办全部作业。老师和家长需要讨论准确性与隐私。目标应是辅助学习，而不是绕过学习本身的努力。

关键词

with guidance · basic skills · learning with assistance

How can AI help in our lives?

Do you think students are overly reliant on AI?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Some students may become overly dependent on AI if they use it to produce answers without understanding them. That makes an assignment look complete while the actual learning remains weak. However, dependence is not inevitable. Asking for feedback after making an independent attempt can be useful. The key is whether the student is still doing the thinking.

中文对照

有些学生只让AI生成答案、不理解内容，就可能过度依赖。作业看似完成，实际学习却薄弱。但依赖并非必然：独立尝试后寻求反馈可能很有用。关键在于学生是否仍在自己思考。

关键词

overly dependent · an independent attempt · actual learning

遇到的科技问题

原题与完整提示

What can teachers do to stop students from relying too much on AI?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Teachers can ask students to show their reasoning and explain answers in their own words. Short classroom tasks also reveal what they can do without assistance. Instead of only banning AI, teachers should explain acceptable uses and design work that values the process. Feedback on drafts can then support learning without allowing the tool to replace every stage.

中文对照

老师可以要求学生展示推理、用自己的话解释答案。课堂短任务也能显示他们在没有辅助时会做什么。与其只禁AI，不如说明合理用法，设计重视过程的任务。对草稿提供反馈，则能辅助学习而不替代所有环节。

关键词

show reasoning · acceptable uses · the learning process

What are the advantages and disadvantages of advertisements?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

中文对照

关键词

discover products · misleading claims · useful information

Why are many advertisements endorsed by celebrities? How useful are they?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

中文对照

关键词

attract attention · brand awareness · fits the product

Why are some advertisements boring?

关键词

名人出演的广告

原题与完整提示

Which is more effective, online advertising or offline advertising?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Online advertising can reach specific groups and make it easy to find more information or buy immediately. Offline advertising may work better in a local area or for people who use the internet less. Neither is always more effective. The right choice depends on the audience, the product and the message, and sometimes combining the two is sensible.

中文对照

线上广告可以触达特定人群，也方便立即查信息或购买。线下广告可能更适合本地地区或较少上网的人。没有一种总是更有效，应根据受众、产品和信息选择，有时结合两种很合理。

关键词

specific groups · a local area · combine the two

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-276 · v01-full

包含动物的故事或书

原题与完整提示

Many people regard pets as members of their family. What do you think?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

I can understand that view because people form routines and emotional connections with animals they care for. A pet may provide companionship and become part of everyday family life. However, treating it as family should also mean respecting its actual needs. Animals should not be expected to behave like people simply because their owners feel close to them.

中文对照

我理解这种看法，因为人们会与自己照料的动物形成日常习惯和情感联系。宠物提供陪伴，成为家庭日常的一部分。但把它当家人也应尊重它真正的需求，不能因为亲近，就要求动物像人一样行为。

关键词

companionship · emotional connections · actual needs

包含动物的故事或书

原题与完整提示

Why do people always tell children stories with animals?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Animals make stories easy for children to imagine and remember. Different characters can represent feelings or behaviours without making the lesson feel too direct. A story about a helpful rabbit, for example, can introduce kindness. However, children should eventually learn that real animals have their own needs and do not behave exactly like the human characters in stories.

中文对照

动物使故事更容易让孩子想象和记住。不同角色可以表现情感或行为，又不让道理显得太直接。例如，热心兔子的故事能引出善良。不过，孩子最终也应了解真实动物有自身需求，并不完全像故事里的人格化角色。

关键词

imagine and remember · kindness · real animals

原题与完整提示

What kinds of rules do schools in China have?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Schools in China commonly have rules about attendance, classroom behaviour, safety and academic honesty. Some also set expectations for uniforms or the use of phones. The details differ between schools. I think the strongest rules are those with a clear purpose, such as preventing bullying, and students should understand the reason rather than follow them only because punishment is possible.

中文对照

中国学校通常有出勤、课堂行为、安全和学术诚信方面的规则，也有学校要求制服或规范手机使用。细节因学校而异。我认为最有力的规则都有明确目的，比如防止欺凌；学生应理解理由，而非仅因为可能受罚才遵守。

关键词

attendance · academic honesty · a clear purpose

原题与完整提示

Do you think school rules are important?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, sensible rules help students learn in a safe and predictable environment. Without them, disruption or bullying may make school unfair for others. However, importance does not mean every existing rule is good. Schools should review rules that create unnecessary restrictions and explain the useful ones, so discipline supports learning rather than becomes an end in itself.

关键词

a predictable environment · unnecessary restrictions · support learning

How can parents and teachers help children understand and follow rules?

What are the rules people should obey at work?

原题与完整提示

What is the purpose of punishment?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Punishment should discourage harmful behaviour, protect others and help the person understand what needs to change. It should be proportionate and fair rather than simply painful. In a school, a consequence can be combined with an explanation and a chance to repair harm. I think prevention and learning are better goals than revenge or public humiliation.

中文对照

惩罚应阻止有害行为、保护他人，帮助当事人理解需要改什么。惩罚应适度公平，而非单纯让人痛苦。在学校，后果可以结合解释和弥补伤害的机会。我认为预防和学习，比报复或公开羞辱更好的目标。

关键词

proportionate · repair harm · prevention and learning

In what situations do people spend a long time responding to others’ messages?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People may reply slowly when they are working, travelling or dealing with something urgent. A message may also need a careful answer rather than a quick response. 中文对照

人们工作、旅行或处理急事时可能回复慢。有些消息需要认真回答，不能匆忙回复；有时通知干脆被漏看。我不会自动把延迟理解为不尊重，因为发信者只看见等了多久，看不见对方生活里的全部情况。

关键词

a careful answer · missed notifications · not automatically disrespect

很久没收到回复的信息

原题与完整提示

In what situations do people not respond to messages at all?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Some messages are spam or irrelevant, so people reasonably choose not to answer. Other messages may feel intrusive, upsetting or difficult to respond to. Forgetfulness can also play a part. If there is an existing commitment, though, a short explanation is usually more considerate than silence, because it tells the other person what to expect.

中文对照

有些消息是垃圾信息或无关内容，人们不回复很合理。另一些可能侵犯边界、令人不快或难以回应，也可能只是忘记了。但如果已有承诺，简短解释通常比沉默体贴，因为能让对方知道接下来如何预期。

关键词

irrelevant messages · an existing commitment · a short explanation

What would you do if you did not receive a reply after sending out a message?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

I would first consider whether the message is urgent and whether I have allowed a reasonable amount of time. If it matters, I would send one polite follow-up or use another appropriate channel. 中文对照

我会先看消息是否紧急、是否已经给了合理时间。如果重要，会礼貌跟进一次，或使用另一个合适渠道。反复发消息可能只制造压力。对不那么重要的事，我会等待，避免认定对方故意不理我。

关键词

reasonable time · a polite follow-up · another channel

原题与完整提示

Why do some people prefer sending a message instead of making a call?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Messages let people respond when they are available, rather than interrupting them immediately. They also provide a written record of details, which is useful for dates or instructions. Some people find it easier to organise their thoughts in writing. However, a call may be better for something urgent or emotionally complicated, where tone and immediate questions matter.

中文对照

消息让人有空时回复，不会立刻打断，也保留细节的文字记录，适合日期或说明。有些人写下来更容易组织想法。不过，紧急或情感复杂的事情可能更适合电话，因为语气和即时提问很重要。

关键词

a written record · organise thoughts · tone

How do you show your respect in your messages?

Why do some people feel angry when others don’t reply to their message?

关键词

feel ignored · clear expectations · personal rejection

Is it good for a person to be ambitious?

Ambition can be positive because it encourages people to set goals and persist through difficulties. It becomes less helpful when the goal is unrealistic or success is defined only by status. 中文对照

关键词

persist through difficulties · healthy ambition · adjust the route

Part 3大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-298 · v01-full

改变重要想法

原题与完整提示

When do most children begin to have their own opinions?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Children begin expressing preferences quite early, but forming a reasoned opinion develops gradually. As they gain language, experience and opportunities to ask questions, they can explain why they think something. I would not give one exact age for everyone. Adults can support the process by listening and asking simple follow-up questions instead of immediately correcting every different view.

中文对照

孩子很早就表达偏好，但有理由的观点是逐渐形成的。语言、经验和提问机会增加后，就能解释为什么这样想。我不会给所有人规定一个精确年龄。成年人可以倾听、简单追问，而不是立即纠正每一种不同看法。

关键词

reasoned opinions · develop gradually · follow-up questions

How does technology affect the law?

Will there be a law that is universally accepted?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

中文对照

关键词

a broad principle · enforcement · common ground

What can we learn from history?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

History helps us understand how present institutions, ideas and conflicts developed. It also gives examples of choices and their consequences. 中文对照

历史帮助理解当前制度、思想和冲突如何形成，也展示选择及其后果。但过去不会在完全一样的条件下重演，因此应谨慎对待简单教训。我认为研究证据和不同视角很有价值，能教人质疑过于轻易的解释。

关键词

institutions and ideas · different perspectives · question explanations

原题与完整提示

Why do some people have a better memory than others?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Memory can differ because of attention, practice, familiarity and the conditions in which people learn. Someone who understands a subject well may connect new information more easily. Sleep and stress can also affect recall. I would not assume a strong memory is simply a fixed talent; useful strategies and repeated practice can improve how information is stored and retrieved.

中文对照

记忆差异可能来自注意力、练习、熟悉程度和学习条件。理解某领域的人更容易连接新信息，睡眠和压力也影响回忆。我不会认为好记忆只是固定天赋；有效策略和反复练习能改善信息储存与提取。

关键词

attention and practice · connect information · retrieve

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-321 · v01-full

想再去一次的城市

原题与完整提示

What should the government do to improve citizens' safety?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Governments should make public spaces, transport and emergency services reliable. Good lighting, safer road design and clear reporting channels can prevent problems before they become serious. Enforcement should also be fair and consistent. I think safety depends on practical prevention and public trust, rather than only stricter punishment after harm has already occurred.

中文对照

政府应保障公共空间、交通和应急服务可靠。良好照明、安全道路设计和清晰报告渠道，能在问题严重前预防它们。执法也应公平一致。我认为安全依赖实际预防和公众信任，而非只在伤害发生后加重惩罚。

关键词

emergency services · prevention · public trust

原题与完整提示

Are there any unlucky things that people should not buy as gifts in your country?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Some gifts have cultural associations that people may consider unlucky. In China, a clock can be sensitive because the phrase for giving one sounds like a phrase connected with a funeral. However, beliefs vary between people and generations. I would consider the recipient's preferences rather than assume every cultural association is a strict rule for everyone.

中文对照

有些礼物有可能被视为不吉利的文化联想。在中国，钟可能敏感，因为“送钟”的读音接近与葬礼有关的说法。但观念因人和代际而异。我会考虑收礼者偏好，而非假设所有文化联想都对人人构成严格禁忌。

关键词

cultural associations · a clock · the recipient's preferences

What can we do to reduce the possibility of losing things?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

中文对照

关键词

a consistent place · check before leaving · a clear routine

Is it effective to offer rewards to find lost things?

关键词

an additional incentive · contact information · return the item

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-343 · v01-full

公共场所丢物品

原题与完整提示

What will people do when losing things?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People usually retrace their steps and check the places where they last used the item. They may ask staff, contact a lost-property office or notify someone who can help. If the item contains sensitive information, protecting that information is also important. A calm, organised search is usually more useful than repeatedly looking in the same place without a plan.

中文对照

人们通常回走走过的路，检查最后用过物品的地方，也会问工作人员、联系失物处，或找能帮忙的人。若物品含敏感信息，也需保护信息。冷静、有条理地找，比没有计划地反复查同一处更有效。

关键词

retrace steps · lost-property office · an organised search

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-344 · v01-full

公共场所丢物品

原题与完整提示

What kinds of things do people usually lose?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Small items that people carry and use frequently are easy to misplace, such as keys, phones, wallets or headphones. They may be put down briefly and forgotten when attention shifts. Documents or tickets can also be lost during travel. The pattern is often about movement and distraction, so having a designated place for essential items can reduce the risk.

中文对照

经常携带使用的小物件容易放错，比如钥匙、手机、钱包或耳机。人们可能暂放后因注意转移而忘掉，旅途中也会丢文件或票。这往往与移动和分心有关，因此给重要物品固定位置能降低风险。

关键词

frequently used items · attention shifts · a designated place

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-345 · v01-full

节约时间的方法/改变

原题与完整提示

What can people do to save time?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

People can decide priorities, group similar tasks and reduce unnecessary interruptions. Preparing common items in advance also prevents repeated searching. However, saving time should not mean rushing every activity. Some tasks need patience and careful checking. I think the aim is to spend less time on avoidable friction so there is more time for the work and rest that matter.

中文对照

人们可以确定优先级、集中处理相似任务、减少无谓打断。提前备好常用物品也省去反复寻找。但省时不意味着凡事赶，有些任务需要耐心和认真检查。目标应是减少可避免的阻碍，为重要工作和休息留时间。

关键词

group tasks · reduce interruptions · avoid unnecessary friction

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-346 · v01-full

节约时间的方法/改变

原题与完整提示

Does technology help people save time? How and why?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Technology can save time by automating routine steps, finding information quickly and helping people coordinate without travelling. For example, a shared document can reduce repeated messages. However, new tools also take time to learn, and notifications can interrupt concentration. The saving is real only when the tool solves a useful problem rather than creates extra work.

中文对照

技术通过自动处理常规步骤、快速找信息、免出行协调来省时。例如，共享文档减少反复发消息。但新工具也需要学习时间，通知还会打断专注。只有工具解决有用问题、而非增加工作时，省时才真实。

关键词

routine steps · a shared document · extra work

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-347 · v01-full

节约时间的方法/改变

原题与完整提示

Do you think parents should be responsible for teaching children to save time?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Parents should help children learn basic time habits, but the responsibility can be shared with schools. Simple routines and realistic expectations work better than constant pressure to be productive. Parents can show how to prepare for an activity and allow enough time. Children also need play and rest, so managing time should include balance rather than eliminating every idle moment.

中文对照

家长应帮助孩子学基本时间习惯，但学校也可分担。简单规律、现实要求，比持续逼孩子高效更好。家长能示范如何准备活动、留足时间。孩子也需要玩耍和休息，因此时间管理应包含平衡，而非消灭所有空闲。

关键词

realistic expectations · simple routines · play and rest

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-348 · v01-full

节约时间的方法/改变

原题与完整提示

Do people who can manage time well become successful more easily?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Good time management can help people make consistent progress and meet commitments, so it may improve their chances of success. But it is not the only factor. Skills, resources, opportunities and unexpected events also matter. Someone can organise time well and still face serious obstacles, so I would not judge success or failure solely by a person's schedule.

中文对照

良好时间管理帮助持续进步、履行承诺，因此可能增加成功机会，但并非唯一因素。技能、资源、机会和意外事件也重要。安排得好仍可能遇到严重障碍，所以我不会只凭日程判断成功或失败。

关键词

consistent progress · opportunities · not the only factor

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-349 · v01-full

节约时间的方法/改变

原题与完整提示

Is it necessary for children to learn time management?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Yes, children benefit from learning to prepare, estimate how long a task takes and remember commitments. These skills can make daily life less stressful. The teaching should match their age and leave room for mistakes. I would not expect a young child to follow an adult's strict timetable, because learning independence takes practice and support.

中文对照

需要。孩子学会准备、估计任务时长、记住承诺，能降低日常压力。教学应符合年龄，允许犯错。我不会要求小孩子遵守成人的严格时间表，因为独立需要练习和支持。

关键词

estimate time · remember commitments · practice and support

Part 3非大陆QB-20261008-01-P3-350 · v01-full

节约时间的方法/改变

原题与完整提示

What can schools do to help students learn time management?

英文参考答案（非用户原答）

Schools can divide larger assignments into stages and show students how to plan each step. Calendars, short checklists and reflection on missed deadlines can be useful. Teachers should also coordinate workload so expectations are realistic. The aim is not simply to punish lateness, but to help students recognise priorities and make a plan they can actually follow.

中文对照

学校可以把大作业分阶段，示范如何规划每一步。日历、简短清单和对错过截止日期的反思都能帮助。老师也应协调工作量，让要求现实。目标不只是罚迟交，而是帮助识别优先级，做出真正能执行的计划。

关键词

divide into stages · realistic workload · a workable plan

待确认素材与观点
